## Classification and safety

- OPEN PRs remain active candidates. PR #3052 remains the single authorized Arvin working lane for this audit; no PR was merged or closed.
- All 1,138 closed PRs are historical records; closed-unmerged does not by itself prove superseded or safe to delete.
- Reused head branch names are duplicate-purpose candidates. Compare exact SHAs, files, and downstream PRs before proposing any closure.
- CI and review state for every PR except #3052 were not individually expanded in this audit snapshot; do not infer PASS from open/closed state.
